// we rely on stable JSON representation of the request (field order preserved).\n   372→    let j = json!({\n   373→        \"page\": req.page,\n   374→        \"per_page\": req.per_page,\n   375→        \"from\": req.from,\n   376→        \"to\": req.to\n   377→    });\n   378→    hasher.update(j.to_string().as_bytes());\n   379→    format!(\"ledger_entries:{}:{}\", tenant_id,    pub async fn get_ledger_entries(\n   396→        &self,\n   397→        tenant_id: Uuid,\n   398→        mut req: LedgerEntryPageRequest,\n   399→    ) -> ServiceResult<LedgerEntryPageResponse> {\n Module 43 – Ledger Entry paging/query service with transparent response caching.\n//!\n//! This file is intentionally self-contained so it can be reviewed in isolation, yet it follows the\n//! same architectural boundaries that the `LedgerLink Nexus` project establishes globally:\n//! • View  <—>  View-Model (DTO)  <—>  Domain Model  <—>  Repository  <—>  Storage\n//!\n//! The code below wires together.\n//!  1. DTOs with payload validation (`validator`).\n//!  2. Domain model describing a ledger entry.\n//!  3. Repository abstraction + (feature-gated) Postgres implementation (`sqlx`).\n//!  4. Redis-backed, JSON-encoded response cache for deterministic pagination.\n//!  5. Service layer that orchestrates repository & cache with robust error handling.\n//!  6. Unit tests exercising critical paths, including cache hits/misses.\n//!\n//! Any I/O layers (REST/GraphQL controllers) only depend on the public service API, so upgrading\n//! persistence or caching strategies does not leak through to transport layers.\n\n#![allow(clippy::missing_panics_doc)]\n\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse rust_decimal::Decimal;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse in the general ledger.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct LedgerEntry {\n    pub id: Uuid,\n    pub tenant_id: Uuid,\n    pub account_id: String,\n    pub amount: Decimal,\n    pub currency: String,\n    pub entry_date: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub description: Option<String>,\n}\n\n/// ------------- View-Model / DTO ----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/// Custom validator – page size must be power-of-two.\nfn validate_pow2(val: &i64) -> Result<(), ValidationError> {\n    if *val >= 1 && val.is_power_of_two() {\n        Ok(())\n    } else {\n        Err(ValidationError::new(\"page_size_pow_2\"))\n    }\n}\n\n/// Caller-supplied paging/filtering options.\n#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Validate)]\npub struct LedgerEntryPageRequest {\n    #[validate(range(min = 1, max = 1_000_000))]\n    pub page: i64,\n\n    #[validate(custom = \"validate_pow2\")]\n    pub per_page: i64,\n\n    /// Optional earliest entry date (inclusive)\n    pub from: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,\n    /// Optional latest entry date (exclusive)\n    pub to: Option<DateTime<Utc>>,\n}\n\n/// Pagination meta-data that callers often need for UI widgets.\n#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\npub struct PaginationMeta {\n    pub page: i64,\n    pub per_page: i64,\n    pub total: i64,\n    pub has_more: bool,\n}\n\n/// Ledger entry projection exposed over the wire (redacted DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub description: Option<String>,\n}\n\n/// Paged response DTO.\n#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\npub struct LedgerEntryPageResponse {\n    pub data: Vec<LedgerEntryView>,\n    pub meta: PaginationMeta,\n}\n\n/// Helper conversions\nimpl From<LedgerEntry> for LedgerEntryView {\n    fn from(m: LedgerEntry) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            id: m.id,\n            account_id: m.account_id,\n            amount: m.amount,\n            currency: m.currency,\n            entry_date: m.entry_date,\n            description: m.description,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// ------------- Repository layer ----------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum RepositoryError {\n    #[error(\"database error: {0}\")]\n    Db(#[from] sqlx::Error),\n\n    #[error(\"unknown repository error: {0}\")]\n    Other(#[from] anyhow::Error),\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\npub trait LedgerEntryRepository: Send + Sync {\n    async fn count_entries(\n        &self,\n        tenant_id: Uuid,\n        filter: &LedgerEntryPageRequest,\n    ) -> Result<i64, RepositoryError>;\n\n    async fn fetch_entries(\n        &self,\n        tenant_id: Uuid,\n        filter: &LedgerEntryPageRequest,\n        offset: i64,\n        limit: i64,\n    ) -> Result<Vec<LedgerEntry>, RepositoryError>;\n}\n\n/// Real Postgres implementation. It is feature-gated because different deployments might swap\n/// this for a DynamoDB or CockroachDB backend without recompiling dependents.\n#[cfg(feature = \"postgres\")]\npub mod postgres_repository {\n    use super::*;\n    use sqlx::{postgres::PgRow, PgPool, Row};\n\n    pub struct PgLedgerEntryRepository {\n        pool: PgPool,\n    }\n\n    impl PgLedgerEntryRepository {\n        pub fn new(pool: PgPool) -> Self {\n            Self { pool }\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[async_trait]\n    impl LedgerEntryRepository for PgLedgerEntryRepository {\n        async fn count_entries(\n            &self,\n            tenant_id: Uuid,\n            filter: &LedgerEntryPageRequest,\n        ) -> Result<i64, RepositoryError> {\n            let mut query = String::from(\n                \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ledger_entries \\\n                 WHERE tenant_id = $1\",\n            );\n\n            if filter.from.is_some() {\n                query.push_str(\" AND entry_date >= $2\");\n            }\n            if filter.to.is_some() {\n                query.push_str(\" AND entry_date < $3\");\n            }\n\n            let row: (i64,) = match (filter.from, filter.to) {\n                (Some(from), Some(to)) => {\n                    sqlx::query_as(query.as_str())\n                        .bind(tenant_id)\n                        .bind(from)\n                        .bind(to)\n                        .fetch_one(&self.pool)\n                        .await?\n                }\n                (Some(from), None) => {\n                    sqlx::query_as(query.as_str())\n                        .bind(tenant_id)\n                        .bind(from)\n                        .fetch_one(&self.pool)\n                        .await?\n                }\n                (None, Some(to)) => {\n                    sqlx::query_as(query.as_str())\n                        .bind(tenant_id)\n                        .bind(to)\n                        .fetch_one(&self.pool)\n                        .await?\n                }\n                (None, None) => {\n                    sqlx::query_as(query.as_str())\n                        .bind(tenant_id)\n                        .fetch_one(&self.pool)\n                        .await?\n                }\n            };\n\n            Ok(row.0)\n        }\n\n        async fn fetch_entries(\n            &self,\n            tenant_id: Uuid,\n            filter: &LedgerEntryPageRequest,\n            offset: i64,\n            limit: i64,\n        ) -> Result<Vec<LedgerEntry>, RepositoryError> {\n            let mut sql = String::from(\n                \"SELECT id, tenant_id, account_id, amount, currency, entry_date, description \\\n                 FROM ledger_entries WHERE tenant_id = $1\",\n            );\n\n            if filter.from.is_some() {\n                sql.push_str(\" AND entry_date >= $2\");\n            }\n            if filter.to.is_some() {\n                sql.push_str(\" AND entry_date < $3\");\n            }\n            sql.push_str(\" ORDER BY entry_date DESC\");\n            sql.push_str(\" OFFSET $4 LIMIT $5\");\n\n            // Build dynamic binding list.\n            let rows: Vec<PgRow> = match (filter.from, filter.to) {\n                (Some(from), Some(to)) => {\n                    sqlx::query(sql.as_str())\n                        .bind(tenant_id)\n                        .bind(from)\n                        .bind(to)\n                        .bind(offset)\n                        .bind(limit)\n                        .fetch_all(&self.pool)\n                        .await?\n                }\n                (Some(from), None) => {\n                    sqlx::query(sql.as_str())\n                        .bind(tenant_id)\n                        .bind(from)\n                        .bind(offset)\n                        .bind(limit)\n                        .fetch_all(&self.pool)\n                  .bind(offset)\n                        .bind(limit)\n                        .fetch_all(&self.pool)\n                        .await?\n                }\n                (None, None) .fetch_all(&self.pool)\n                        .await?\n                }\n            };\n\n            let entries = rows\n                .into_iter()\n                .map(|row| LedgerEntry {\n                    id: row.get(\"id\"),\n                    tenant_id: row.get(\"tenant_id\"),\n                    account_id: row.get(\"account_id\"),\n                    amount:          })\n                .collect();\n\n            Ok(entries)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// ------------- Cache layer ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum CacheError {\n    #[error(\"redis error: {0}\")]\n    Redis(#[from] redis::RedisError),\n\n    #[error(\"serialization error: {0}\")]\n    Serde(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n\n    #[error(\"unknown cache error: {0}\")]\n    Other(#[from] anyhow::Error),\n}\n\n/// Publicly consumed cache abstraction (keeps service unit-testable).\n#[async_trait]\npub trait CacheLayer: Send + Sync {\n    async fn get_page(&self, cache_key: &str) -> Result<Option<String>, CacheError>;\n    async fn set_page(&self, cache_key: &str, payload: &str, ttl_secs: usize) -> Result<(), CacheError>;\n}\n\n/// Redis implementation\npub struct RedisCacheLayer {\n    client: redis::Client,\n}\n\nimpl RedisCacheLayer {\n    pub fn new(client: redis::Client) -> Self {\n        Self { client }\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl CacheLayer for RedisCacheLayer {\n    async fn get_page(&self, cache_key: &str) -> Result<Option<String>, CacheError> {\n        let mut conn = self.client.get_async_connection().await?;\n        let val: Option<String> = redis::cmd(\"GET\").arg(cache_key).query_async(&mut conn).await?;\n        Ok(val)\n    }\n\n    async fn set_page(&self, cache_key: &str, payload: &str, ttl_secs: usize) -> Result<(), CacheError> {\n        let mut conn = self.client.get_async_connection().await?;\n        redis::pipe()\n            .cmd(\"SET\")\n            .arg(cache_key)\n            .arg(payload)\n            .cmd(\"EXPIRE\")\n            .arg(cache_key)\n            .arg(ttl_secs)\n            .query_async(&mut conn)\n            .await?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n/// ------------- Service layer -------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum ServiceError {\n    #[error(\"validation error: {0}\")]\n    Validation(#[from] validator::ValidationErrors),\n\n    #[error(\"repository error: {0}\")]\n    Repo(#[from] RepositoryError),\n\n    #[error(\"cache error: {0}\")]\n    Cache(#[from] CacheError),\n}\n\npub type ServiceResult<T> = Result<T, ServiceError>;\n\n/// TTL for paginated cache responses (in seconds)\nconst PAGE_CACHE_TTL_SECS: usize = 30;\n\n/// Stateless cache key generator – deterministic and collision-resistant.\nfn compute_cache_key(tenant_id: Uuid, req: &LedgerEntryPageRequest) -> String {\n    let mut hasher = Sha256::new();\n    // we rely on stable JSON representation of the request (field order preserved).\n    let j = json!({\n        \"page\": req.page,\n        \"per_page\": req.per_page,\n        \"from\": req.from,\n        \"to\": req.to\n    });\n    hasher.update(j.to_string().as_bytes());\n    format!(\"ledger_entries:{}:{}\", tenant_id, hex::encode(hasher.finalize()))\n}\n\n/// High-level query service.\npub struct LedgerEntryQueryService<R: LedgerEntryRepository, C: CacheLayer> {\n    repo: Arc<R>,\n    cache: Arc<C>,\n}\n\nimpl<R: LedgerEntryRepository, C: CacheLayer> LedgerEntryQueryService<R, C> {\n    pub fn new(repo: Arc<R>, cache: Arc<C>) -> Self {\n        Self { repo, cache }\n    }\n\n    /// Fetches a page of ledger entries, relying on Redis as a read-through cache.\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    pub async fn get_ledger_entries(\n        &self,\n        tenant_id: Uuid,\n        mut req: LedgerEntryPageRequest,\n    ) -> ServiceResult<LedgerEntryPageResponse> {\n        // If the consumer forgot to set page/per_page we'll default responsibly.\n        if req.page == 0 {\n            req.page = 1;\n        }\n        if req.per_page == 0 {\n            req.per_page = 32;\n        }\n\n        // Validate.\n        req.validate()?;\n\n        let cache_key = compute_cache_key(tenant_id, &req);\n        if let Some(cached) = self.cache.get_page(&cache_key).await? {\n            trace!(\"cache_hit key={}\", &cache_key);\n            let resp: LedgerEntryPageResponse = serde_json::from_str(&cached)?;\n            return Ok(resp);\n        }\n        trace!(\"cache_miss key={}\", &cache_key);\n\n        let total = self.repo.count_entries(tenant_id, &req).await?;\n        let offset = (req.page - 1) * req.per_page;\n        let limit = req.per_page;\n\n        let models = self\n            .repo\n            .fetch_entries(tenant_id, &req, offset, limit)\n            .await?;\n\n        let data: Vec<LedgerEntryView> = models.into_iter().map(Into::into).collect();\n\n        let resp = LedgerEntryPageResponse {\n            meta: PaginationMeta {\n                page: req.page,\n                per_page: req.per_page,\n                total,\n                has_more: (req.page * req.per_page) < total,\n            },\n            data,\n        };\n\n        // Fire-and-forget caching: do not let errors bubble up to caller.\n        match serde_json::to_string(&resp)\n            .and_then(|payload| {\n                let cache = self.cache.clone();\n                tokio::spawn(async move {\n warn!(\"serialization failed for cacheable response: {}\", e),\n        }\n\n        Ok(resp)\n    }\n}\n\n/// ------------- In-mem stub implementations for unit testing LedgerLink Nexus supports both page-number & cursor styles, however the\n    //! latter is recommended for high-volume tables because it is stable with\n    //! concurrent inserts.  We expose a minimal but production-ready subset\n    //! here.\n\n    use super::*;\n    use serde::de::Error as DeError;\n    use serde::{Deserializer, Serializer};\n\n    /// Input parameters from the client.  Either `after` or `before` acts as a\n    /// cursor (opaque to the caller), combined with a positive `limit`.\n    #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n    pub struct PageParams {\n        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n        pub after: Option<String>,\n        #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = \"Option::is_none\")]\n        pub before: Option<String>,\n        #[serde(default = \"PageParams::default_limit\")]\n        pub limit: u32,\n    }\n\n    impl PageParams {\n        const MAX_LIMIT: u32 = 500;\n\n        fn default_limit() -> u32 {\n            100\n        }\n\n        /// Sanity-check – guarantee reasonable values before we hit the DB.\n        pub fn validate(&self) -> Result<(), PageError> {\n            if self.after.is_some() && self.before.is_some() {\n                return Err(PageError::AmbiguousCursors);\n            }\n            if self.limit == 0 || self.limit > Self::MAX_LIMIT {\n                return Err(PageError::InvalidLimit(self.limit));\n            }\n            Ok(())\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[derive(Debug, Error)]\n    pub enum PageError {\n        #[error(\"both 'after' and 'before' cursors supplied – choose one\")]\n        AmbiguousCursors,\n        #[error(\"limit of {0} outside allowed range\")]\n        InvalidLimit(u32),\n    }\n\n    /// Outgoing envelope – ---------------------------------------------------\n\n    fn serialize_cursor_opt<S>(value: &Option<Vec<u8>>, ser: S) -> Result<S::Ok, S::Error>\n    where\n        S: Serializer,\n    {\n        match value {\n            Some(bytes) => ser.serialize_some(&base64::encode(bytes)),\n            None => ser.serialize_none(),\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn deserialize_cursor_opt<'de, D>(de: D) -> Result<Option<Vec<u8>>, D::Error>\n    where\n        D: Deserializer<'de>,\n    {\n        let opt: Option<String> = Option::deserialize(de)?;\n        opt.map(|s| base64::decode(s).map_err(DeError::custom))\n            .transpose()\n    }\n\n    impl<T> Paged<T> {\n        pub fn empty() -> Self {\n            Self {\n                data: Vec::new(),\n and performs cross-field validation.\n    pub fn normalize(mut self) -> Result<Self, ServiceError> {\n        self.page_size.get_or_insert(DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE);\n        self.page.get_or_insert(1);\n\n        // Ensure `from_date` comes before `to_date`.\n        if let (Some(from), Some(to)) = (self.from_date, self.to_date) {\n            if from > to {\n                return Err(ServiceError::Validation(\n                    \"from_date must not be after to_date\".into(),\n                ));\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(self)\n    }\n\n    /// Calculates SQL OFFSET based on (page, page_size).\n    pub fn offset(&self) -> u32 {\n        (self.page.unwrap_or(1) - 1) * self.page_size.unwrap_or(DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE)\n    }\n\n    /// Calculates SQL LIMIT.\n    pub fn limit(&self) -> u32 {\n        self.page_size.unwrap_or(DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE)\n    }\n}\n\n/* ---------- Paginated envelope  ---------- */\n\n#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\npub struct Paginated<T> {\n    pub items:     Vec<T>,\n    pub page:      u32,\n    pub page_size: u32,\n    pub total:     u64,\n    pub has_next:  bool,\n}\n\n/* ---------- Error layer  ---------- */\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum ServiceError {\n    #[error(\"validation failed: {0}\")]\n    Validation(String),\n    #[error(\"resource not found\")]\n    NotFound,\n    #[error(\"repository error: {0}\")]\n    Repository(#[from] sqlx::Error),\n    #[error(\"cache error: {0}\")]\n    Cache(#[from] redis::RedisError),\n    #[error(\"unknown error: {0}\")]\n    Unknown(String),\n}\n\n/* ---------- Repository connection-pooling options.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait LedgerEntryRepository: Send + Sync {\n    async fn fetch_page(\n        &self,\n        query: &LedgerEntryQuery,\n    ) -> Result<(Vec<LedgerEntryView>, u64 /* total rows */), ServiceError>;\n}\n\n/// Postgres implementation using sqlx.\n///\n/// NOTE:   A separate migration step is assumed to have created an index on\n///         (account_id, posted_at DESC) to satisfy this query efficiently.\npub struct PgLedgerEntryRepository {\n    pool: PgPool,\n}\n\nimpl PgLedgerEntryRepository {\n    pub fn new(pool: PgPool) -> Self {\n        Self { pool }\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl LedgerEntryRepository for PgLedgerEntryRepository {\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    async fn fetch_page(\n        &self,\n        query: &LedgerEntryQuery,\n    ) -> Result<(Vec<LedgerEntryView>, u64), ServiceError> {\n        let timer = Instant::now();\n\n        // Build dynamic filter predicates.\n        let mut conditions: Vec<&str> = Vec::new();\n        let mut args: Vec<Box<dyn sqlx::Encode<'_, sqlx::Postgres> + Send + Sync>> = Vec::new();\n\n        if let Some(acc_id) = query.account_id {\n            conditions.push(\"account_id = $1\");\n            args.push(Box::new(acc_id));\n        }\n        if let Some(from) = query.from_date {\n            conditions.push(\"posted_at >= $2\");\n            args.push(Box::new(from));\n        }\n        if let Some(to) = query.to_date {\n            conditions.push(\"posted_at <= $3\");\n            args.push(Box::new(to.succ_opt().unwrap_or(to))); // inclusive\n        }\n\n        let where_clause = if conditions.is_empty() {\n            \"\".to_string()\n        } else {\n            format!(\"WHERE {}\", conditions.join(\" AND \"))\n        };\n\n        // Count total rows for pagination meta.\n        let count_stmt = format!(\"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ledger_entries {}\", where_clause);\n        let total: (i64,) = sqlx::query_as_with(&count_stmt, args.clone())\n            .fetch_one(&self.pool)\n            .await\n            .map_err(ServiceError::Repository)?;\n\n        // Main SELECT page.\n        let limit = query.limit() as i64;\n        let offset = query.offset() as i64;\n        let select_stmt = format!(\n            \"SELECT id, account_id, amount, currency, posted_at, memo \\\n             FROM ledger_entries {} \\\n             ORDER BY posted_at DESC \\\n             LIMIT $4 OFFSET $5\",\n            where_clause\n        );\n\n        // Append pagination args\n        args.push(Box::new(limit));\n        args.push(Box::new(offset));\n\n        // We need to transmute Box<dyn ...> to actual values; easiest path is to\n        // re-build query with `sqlx::query` binding in sequence.\n        let mut q = sqlx::query(&select_stmt);\n        for (idx, arg) in args.into_iter().enumerate() {\n            q = q.bind(arg); // SAFE: sqlx uses runtime type checking\n        }\n\n        let rows: Vec<PgRow> = q\n            .fetch_all(&self.pool)\n            .await\n            .map_err(ServiceError::Repository)?;\n\n        let entries = rows\n            .into_iter()\n            .map(|row| LedgerEntryView {\n                id: row.get(\"id\"),\n                account_id: row.get(\"account_id\"),\n                amount: row.get(\"amount\"),\n                currency: row.get::<&str, _>(\"currency\").to_owned(),\n                posted_at: row.get(\"posted_at\"),\n                memo: row.get::<Option<&str>, _>(\"memo\").map(ToOwned::to_owned),\n            })\n            .collect();\n\n        debug!(\n            took_ms = timer.elapsed().as_millis() as u64,\n            \"LedgerEntryRepository::fetch_page executed\"\n        );\n\n        Ok((entries, total.0 as u64))\n    }\n}\n\n/* ---------- Cache provider  ---------- */\n\n#[async_trait]\npub trait CacheProvider: Send + Sync {\n    async fn get_cached_page(\n        &self,\n        cache_key: &str,\n    ) -> Result<Option<Paginated<LedgerEntryView>>, ServiceError>;\n\n    async fn cache_page(\n        &self,\n        cache_key: &str,\n        payload: &Paginated<LedgerEntryView>,\n    ) -> Result<(), ServiceError>;\n}\n\n/// Simple JSON-encoded Redis cache.\npub struct RedisCacheProvider {\n    client: async fn get_cached_page(\n        &self,\n        cache_key: &str,\n    ) -> Result<Option<Paginated<LedgerEntryView>>, ServiceError> {\n        let mut conn = self.client.get_async_connection().await?;\n        let data: Option<String> = conn.get(cache_key).await?;\n        match data {\n            Some(raw) => {\n                let page: Paginated<LedgerEntryView> =\n                    serde_json::from_str(&raw).map_err(|e| ServiceError::Unknown(e.to_string()))?;\n                Ok(Some(page))\n            }\n            None => Ok(None),\n        }\n    }\n\n    async fn cache_page(\n        &self,\n        cache_key: &str,\n        payload: &Paginated<LedgerEntryView>,\n    ) -> Result<(), ServiceError> {\n        let mut conn = self.client.get_async_connection().await?;\n        let serialized = serde_json::to_string(payload)\n            .map_err(|e| ServiceError::Unknown(e.to_string()))?;\n        conn.set_ex(cache_key, serialized, CACHE_TTL_SECONDS)\n            .await\n            .map_err(ServiceError::Cache)\n    }\n}\n\n/* ---------- Service layer  ---------- */\n\npub struct LedgerEntryService<R: LedgerEntryRepository, C: CacheProvider> {\n    repo:  R,\n    cache: C,\n}\n\nimpl<R: LedgerEntryRepository, C: CacheProvider> LedgerEntryService<R, C> {\n    pub fn new(repo: R, cache: C) -> Self {\n        Self { repo, cache }\n    }\n\n    /// Returns a paginated collection, using Redis as read-through cache.\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    pub async fn get_paginated(\n        &self,\n        raw_query: LedgerEntryQuery,\n    ) -> Result<Paginated<LedgerEntryView>, ServiceError> {\n        // (1) Validate & normalize\n        let query = raw_query\n            .validate()\n            .map_err(|e| ServiceError::Validation(e.to_string()))?\n            .normalize()?;\n\n        // (2) Compute cache key\n        let cache_key = {\n            let mut hasher = Sha256::new();\n            hasher.update(b\"ledger_entries:\");\n            hasher.update(serde_json::to_vec(&query).unwrap());\n            format!(\"{:x}\", hasher.finalize())\n        };\n\n        // (3) Attempt cache hit\n        if let Some(page) = self.cache.get_cached_page(&cache_key).await? {\n            debug!(\"cache hit for {}\", cache_key);\n            return Ok(page);\n        }\n\n        debug!(\"cache miss for {}\", cache_key);\n\n        // (4) Fetch from repository\n        let (items, total) = self.repo.fetch_page(&query).await?;\n\n        let page = Paginated {\n            items,\n            page: query.page.unwrap(),\n            page_size: query.page_size.unwrap(),\n            total,\n            has_next: (query.offset() as u64 + query.page_size.unwrap() as u64) < total,\n        };\n\n        // (5) Store in cache (fire-and-forget; don't hold client hostage)\n        let cache = self.cache.cache_page(&cache_key, &page);\n        tokio::spawn(async move {\n            if let Err(e) = cache.await {\n                error!(\"Failed to cache page: {:?}\", e);\n            }\n        });\n\n        Ok(page)\n    }\n}\n\n/* Cash-Flow Forecast query service with optional Redis-backed response\n//! caching, pagination helpers and domain-driven abstractions.\n//!\n//! This module is completely self-contained yet showcases the LedgerLink\n//! Nexus architecture principles:\n//!   • Clean separation between View (DTO)  ➜  View-Model (validated\n//!     transport structs)  ➜  Domain Models / Repository  ➜  Service.\n//!   • Transparent response-caching with Redis using a typed cache key.\n//!   • Structured error handling with `thiserror` and `tracing`.\n//!   • Feature-gated compile options (`response_caching`) for optional\n//!     dependencies, keeping binary size and attack-surface minimal.\n//!\n//! Down-stream HTTP/GraphQL handlers can simply call\n//! `CashFlowForecastService::get_forecast(..)` and receive a fully\n//! validated, paginated & (optionally) cached payload ready to be\n//! serialized by the transport layer.\n\n#![allow(clippy::async_yields_async)] // False-positive for async_trait\n#![cfg_attr(docsrs, feature(doc_cfg))]\n\nuse std::{num::NonZeroU32, sync::Arc, NaiveDate,\n    pub to: NaiveDate,\n}\n\nimpl DateRange {\n    pub fn validate(self) -> Result<Self, ValidationError> {\n        if self.from > self.to {\n            Err(ValidationError::InvalidDateRange)\n        } else {\n            Ok(self)\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Simple pagination descriptor (page & per_page are both 1-based).\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct Pagination {\n    page: NonZeroU32,\n    per_page: NonZeroU32,\n}\n\nimpl Pagination {\n    pub fn new(page: u32, per_page: u32) -> Result<Self, ValidationError> {\n        let page = NonZeroU32::new(page).ok_or(ValidationError::InvalidPage)?;\n        let per_page =\n            NonZeroU32::new(per_page).ok_or(ValidationError::InvalidPageSize)?;\n        Ok(Self { page, per_page })\n    }\n\n    #[inline]\n    pub fn offset(&self) -> u32 {\n        (self.page.get() - 1) * self.per_page.get()\n    }\n\n    #[inline]\n    pub fn limit(&self) -> u32 {\n        self.per_page.get()\n    }\n}\n\n/// Domain aggregate representing a single cash-flow forecast entry.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct CashFlowForecast {\n    pub id: Uuid,\n    pub account_id: Uuid,\n    pub date: NaiveDate,\n    pub expected_inflow: Decimal,\n    pub expected_outflow: Uuid,\n    pub date: NaiveDate,\n    pub expected_inflow: Decimal,\n    pub expected_outflow: Decimal,\n    pub currency: String,\n}\n\nimpl From<CashFlowForecast> for CashFlowForecastDto {\n    fn from(f: CashFlowForecast) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            id: f.id,\n            date: f.date,\n            expected_inflow: f.expected_inflow,\n            expected_outflow: f.expected_outflow,\n            currency: f.currency,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Output envelope with pagination metadata.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]\npub struct CashFlowForecastPage {\n    pub items: Vec<CashFlowForecastDto>,\n    pub total_items: u64,\n    pub page: u32,\n    pub per_page: u32,\n}\n\n/// Domain-driven repository abstraction.\n///\n/// Implementations live in separate #[cfg(feature = \"response_caching\")]\n    ttl: Duration,\n}\n\nimpl<R, C> CashFlowForecastService<R, C>\nwhere\n    R: CashFlowForecastRepository,\n{\n    pub fn new(repo: Arc<R>) -> Self\n    where\n        C: Default,\n    {\n        Self {\n            repo,\n            #[cfg(feature = \"response_caching\")]\n            cache: Arc::<C>::default(),\n            #[cfg(feature = #[cfg(feature = \"response_caching\")]\n        if !force_refresh {\n            if let Some(cached) = self\n                .cache\n                .get::<CashFlowForecastPage>(&CacheKey::new(\n                    account_id,\n                    range,\n                    pagination,\n                ))\n                .await?\n            {\n                debug!(\"cache hit for .map_err(ServiceError::Repository)?;\n        debug!(\"db_query completed in {:?}\", started_at.elapsed());\n\n        let page = CashFlowForecastPage {\n            items: #[cfg(feature = \"response_caching\")]\n        {\n            self.cache\n                .set(\n                    CacheKey::new(account_id, range, pagination),\n                    &page,\n                    self.ttl,\n                )\n                .await?;\n        }\n\n        Ok(page)\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// ----------------------------- Caching    B: ConnectionLike + Send + Sync + 'static,\n{\n    async fn get<T>(&self, key: &CacheKey) -> Result<Option<T>, CacheError>\n    where\n        T: for<'de> Deserialize<'de> + Send,\n    {\n              let parsed = serde_json::from_str::<T>(&s)?;\n                Some(parsed)\n            }\n            None => None,\n        })\n    }\n\n    async fn set<T>(\n        &self,\n        key: CacheKey,\n        value: &T,\n        ttl: Duration,\n    ) -> Result<(), CacheError>\n    where\n        T: Serialize + Send + Sync,\n    {\n        let redis_key = key.as_redis_key();\n       .await?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n//\n// ----------------------------- Errors -----------------------------------\n//\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum ValidationError {\n #[error(\"redis error: {0}\")]\n    Redis(#[from] redis::RedisError),\n    #[error(\"serialization error: {0}\")]\n    Serde(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum ServiceError {\n    #[error(\"validation error: {0}\")]\n    Validation(#[from] ValidationError),\n    #[error(\"repository error: {0}\")]\n    Repository(#[from] RepoError),\n    #[cfg(feature = anyhow!(\"Missing `Cache-Control` header\"))?\n        .to_str()\n        .context(\"Parsing `Cache-Control` header\")?;\n\n    if cc.contains(\"no-store\") {\n        // Explicitly opted-out -> we’re good.\n        return Ok(());\n    }\n\n    // Must have a max-age AND an ETag\n    let has_max_age = cc.split(',').any(|d| d.trim().starts_with(\"max-age=\"));\n    let has_etag = resp.headers().contains_key(header::ETAG);\n\n    if has_max_age && has_etag {\n        Ok(())\n    } else {\n        Err(anyhow!(\"Cache headers invalid: {cc:?} / etag={has_etag}\"))\n    }\n}\n\n// -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Authentication helpers\n// -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/// Short-hand to build a *signed* bearer token for test scenarios.\n///\n/// The microservice under test is expected to validate HS256 signed\n/// tokens with an internal **HMAC** secret (`LEDGERLINK_TEST_JWT_KEY`).\n///\n/// NOTE: These *test* secrets **MUST NOT** be used in production!\npub fn bearer_token(user_id: Uuid, roles: &[&str]) -> Result<String> {\n    #[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\n    struct Claims<'a> {\n        sub: Uuid,\n        exp: usize,\n        roles: &'a [&'a str],\n    }\n\n    let exp = SystemTime::now()\n        .checked_add(Duration::from_secs(60 * 60)) // 1h\n        .expect(\"valid timestamp\")\n        .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)\n        .expect(\"Time went backwards\")\n        .as_secs() as usize;\n\n    let claims = Claims {\n        sub: user_id,\n        exp,\n        roles,\n    };\n\n    let secret = std::env::var(\"LEDGERLINK_TEST_JWT_KEY\")\n        .unwrap_or_else(|_| \"ledgerlink-super-secret\".into());\n\n    let header = jwt::Header::default();\n    jwt::encode(&header, &claims, &jwt::EncodingKey::from_secret(secret.as_bytes()))\n        .context(\"Signing test JWT\")\n}\n\n/// Convenience wrapper to attach the `Authorization: Bearer <token>`\n/// header to a `reqwest::RequestBuilder`.\npub trait WithBearer {\n    fn bearer(self, token: &str) -> Self;\n}\n\nimpl WithBearer for reqwest::RequestBuilder {\n    fn bearer(self, token: &str) -> Self {\n        self.header(header::AUTHORIZATION, format!(\"Bearer {token}\"))\n    }\n}\n\n// -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Example integration test (kept here to ensure utils compile)\n// Value = json(resp).await?;\n        assert_eq!(body, json!({ \"status\": \"ok\" }));\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":312,"startLine":1,"totalLines":312}}}

244→        if !auth.can_read_ledger() {\n   245→            error!(\"unauthorised access by user {}\", auth.user_id);\n   246→            return Err(LedgerEntryServiceError::Unauthorised);\n   247→ = ((total_items as f64) / (paging.per_page as f64)).ceil() as u32;\n   290→\n   291→        let response = PagedResult {\n   292→            data: dto_list,\n   293→            pagination: PaginationMeta {\n   294→                page: paging.page,\n   295→                per_page: paging.per_page,\n   296→                total_items,\n   297→                total_pages,\n   298→            },\n   299→            cache: CacheHint::Ttl(ttl_seconds),\n   300→        };\n   301→\n   302→        // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n   303→        // Store in cache for next caller\n   304→        // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n   305→        if let Ok(bytes) = bincode::serialize(&response) {\n   306→            if let Err(e) = self.cache.set_bin(&cache_key, &bytes, ttl_seconds).await {\n   307→                error!(\"failed to set cache {}: {}\", cache_key, e);\n   308→            }\n   309→        }\n   310→\n   311→        info!(\n   312→            \"tenant {} fetched ledger entries page {} ({} items)\",\n   313→            auth.tenant_id, paging.page, response.data.len()\n   314→        );\n inner: Mutex<HashMap<String, Vec<u8>>>,\n   367→    }\n   368→\n   369→    impl InMemCache {\n   370→        fn new() -> Self {\n   371→            Self {\n   372→                inner:    inner: Arc::new(Mutex::new(conn)),\n    49→        })\n    50→    }\n    51→\n    52→    async fn with_conn<T, F>(&self, fut: F) -> Result<T, CacheError>\n    53→    where\n    54→        F:    62→    }\n    63→}\n    64→\n    65→/// Enum describing cache result metadata.\n    66→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]\n    67→pub enum CacheStatus {\n    68→    Hit,\n    69→ 96→    /// Compose a namespaced key: `<prefix>:<tenant>:<path>|<query>`\n    97→    fn compose_key(&self, parts: &[&str]) -> String {\n    98→        let joined = parts.join(\"|\");\n    99→        format!(\"{}:{}\", self.prefix, joined)\n   100→    }\n   101→\n   102→    /// Main entry point – transparently fetches/creates a value.\n   103→    ///\n   104→    /// Example usage inside a   .list_separator(\",\"),\n    88→        );\n    89→\n    90→        // finish\n    91→        cfg.build()\n    92→            .map_err(ConfigLoadError::Parse)?\n    93→ response::{IntoResponse, Response},\n    Json,\n};\nuse serde::Serialize;\nuse thiserror::Error;\n\n/// Structured JSON error response body.\n///\n/// All API errors are serialized in this format:\n/// ```json\n/// {\n///   \"error\": {\n///     \"type\": \"validation\",\n///     \"message\": \"Invalid input provided\"\n///   }\n/// }\n/// ```\n#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\npub struct ErrorResponse {\n    pub error: ErrorBody,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\npub struct ErrorBody {\n    #[serde(rename = \"type\")]\n    pub error_type: String,\n    pub message: String,\n}\n\nimpl ErrorResponse {\n    pub fn new(error_type: impl Into<String>, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            error: ErrorBody {\n                error_type: error_type.into(),\n                message: message.into(),\n            },\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Unified application error type for LedgerLink Nexus.\n///\n/// This enum represents all possible failure modes in the application and\n/// provides automatic conversion from common dependency error types.\n///\n/// # Error Categories\n///\n/// - **Validation**: Input validation failures (HTTP 400)\n/// - **NotFound**: Resource not found (HTTP 404)\n/// - **Unauthorized**: Authentication failures (HTTP 401)\n/// - **Forbidden**: Authorization failures (HTTP 403)\n/// - **Database**: Database operation failures (HTTP 500)\n/// - **Cache**: Caching layer failures (HTTP 500)\n/// - **Serialization**: JSON serialization/deserialization errors (HTTP 500)\n/// - **Internal**: Generic internal server errors (HTTP 500)\n///\n/// # Example\n///\n/// ```rust\n/// use crate::error::AppError;\n///\n/// fn validate_input(id: &str) -> Result<(), AppError> {\n///     if id.is_empty() {\n///         return Err(AppError::ValidationError(\"ID cannot be empty\".to_string()));\n///     }\n///     Ok(())\n/// }\n/// ```\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum AppError {\n    /// Validation error for invalid user input.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 400 Bad Request.\n    #[error(\"Validation Error: {0}\")]\n    ValidationError(String),\n\n    /// Resource not found error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 404 Not Found.\n    #[error(\"Resource Not Found: {0}\")]\n    NotFound(String),\n\n    /// Authentication failure.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 401 Unauthorized.\n    #[error(\"Authentication Failed\")]\n    Unauthorized,\n\n    /// Authorization failure - user lacks permissions.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 403 Forbidden.\n    #[error(\"Access Forbidden: {0}\")]\n    Forbidden(String),\n\n    /// Database operation error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Database Error: {0}\")]\n    Database(#[from] sqlx::Error),\n\n    /// Redis caching error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Cache Error: {0}\")]\n    Cache(#[from] redis::RedisError),\n\n    /// JSON serialization/deserialization error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Serialization Error: {0}\")]\n    Serialization(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n\n    /// Validation errors from the `validator` crate.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 400 Bad Request.\n    #[error(\"Input Validation Failed: {0}\")]\n    ValidatorErrors(#[from] validator::ValidationErrors),\n\n    /// Generic internal server error.\n    /// Use this for unexpected errors that don't fit other categories.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Internal Server Error: {0}\")]\n    Internal(#[from] anyhow::Error),\n\n    /// Pagination error for invalid page/limit parameters.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 400 Bad Request.\n    #[error(\"Pagination Error: {0}\")]\n    PaginationError(String),\n\n    /// Configuration error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Configuration Error: {0}\")]\n    ConfigError(String),\n\n    /// External service error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 502 Bad Gateway.\n    #[error(\"External Service Error: {0}\")]\n    ExternalService(String),\n\n    /// Request timeout error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 504 Gateway Timeout.\n    #[error(\"Request Timeout: {0}\")]\n    Timeout(String),\n\n    /// Rate limit exceeded.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 429 Too Many Requests.\n    #[error(\"Rate Limit Exceeded: {0}\")]\n    RateLimited(String),\n\n    /// Conflict error (e.g., duplicate resource).\n    /// Maps to HTTP 409 Conflict.\n    #[error(\"Conflict: {0}\")]\n    Conflict(String),\n}\n\nimpl AppError {\n    /// Returns the HTTP status code for this error.\n    pub fn status_code(&self) -> StatusCode {\n        match self {\n            AppError::ValidationError(_) => StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,\n            AppError::ValidatorErrors(_) => StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,\n            AppError::PaginationError(_) => StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,\n            AppError::NotFound(_) => StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,\n            AppError::Unauthorized => StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED,\n            AppError::Forbidden(_) => StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,\n            AppError::Conflict(_) => StatusCode::CONFLICT,\n            AppError::RateLimited(_) => StatusCode::TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,\n            AppError::Timeout(_) => StatusCode::GATEWAY_TIMEOUT,\n            AppError::ExternalService(_) => StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY,\n            AppError::Database(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n            AppError::Cache(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n            AppError::Serialization(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n            AppError::Internal(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n            AppError::ConfigError(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the error type string for the JSON response.\n    pub fn error_type(&self) -> &'static str {\n        match self {\n            AppError::ValidationError(_) => \"validation\",\n            AppError::ValidatorErrors(_) => \"validation\",\n            AppError::PaginationError(_) => \"pagination\",\n            AppError::NotFound(_) => \"not_found\",\n            AppError::Unauthorized => \"unauthorized\",\n            AppError::Forbidden(_) => \"forbidden\",\n            AppError::Conflict(_) => \"conflict\",\n            AppError::RateLimited(_) => \"rate_limited\",\n            AppError::Timeout(_) => \"timeout\",\n            AppError::ExternalService(_) => \"external_service\",\n            AppError::Database(_) => \"database\",\n            AppError::Cache(_) => \"cache\",\n            AppError::Serialization(_) => \"serialization\",\n            AppError::Internal(_) => \"internal\",\n            AppError::ConfigError(_) => \"config\",\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a validation error.\n    pub fn validation(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::ValidationError(msg.into())\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a not found error.\n    pub fn not_found(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::NotFound(msg.into())\n    }\n\n    /// Creates an internal error from any error type.\n    pub fn internal(err: impl std::error::Error + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::Error::new(err))\n    }\n\n    /// Creates an internal error from a string message.\n    pub fn internal_msg(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::anyhow!(msg.into()))\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a forbidden error.\n    pub fn forbidden(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::Forbidden(msg.into())\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a pagination error.\n    pub fn pagination(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::PaginationError(msg.into())\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a conflict error.\n    pub fn conflict(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::Conflict(msg.into())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Implement `IntoResponse` for `AppError` to convert errors into HTTP responses.\n///\n/// This enables using `AppError` as the error type in Axum handlers:\n///\n/// ```rust\n/// async fn handler() -> Result<Json<Data>, AppError> {\n///     // ...\n/// }\n/// ```\nimpl IntoResponse for AppError {\n    fn into_response(self) -> Response {\n        // Log the error for debugging (in production, use appropriate log level)\n        tracing::error!(\n            error_type = self.error_type(),\n            error_message = %self,\n            \"API error occurred\"\n        );\n\n        let status = self.status_code();\n        let error_response = ErrorResponse::new(self.error_type(), self.to_string());\n\n        (status, Json(error_response)).into_response()\n    }\n}\n\n/// Convenience type alias for Results using AppError.\npub type AppResult<T> = Result<T, AppError>;\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Additional From implementations for common error types\n// ============================================================================\n\nimpl From<String> for AppError {\n    fn from(msg: String) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::anyhow!(msg))\n    }\n}\n\nimpl From<&str> for AppError {\n    fn from(msg: &str) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::anyhow!(msg.to_string()))\n    }\n}\n\nimpl From<std::io::Error> for AppError {\n    fn from(err: std::io::Error) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::Error::new(err))\n    }\n}\n\nimpl From<Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> for AppError {\n    fn from(err: Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::Error::new(err))\n    }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// Unit \"ledger:entries:{}:p{}:s{}\",\n   225→            tenant, paging.page, paging.per_page\n   226→        )\n   227→    }\n   228→}\n   229→\n   230→#[async_trait]\n   231→impl<R, C> 244→        if !auth.can_read_ledger() {\n   245→            error!(\"unauthorised access by user {}\", auth.user_id);\n   246→            return Err(LedgerEntryServiceError::Unauthorised);\n   247→ 258→        // Attempt cache hit\n   259→        // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n   260→        if let Ok(Some(serialised)) = self.cache.get_bin(&cache_key).await {\n   261→            trace!(\"cache hit for {}\", cache_key);\n   262→            match bincode::deserialize::<PagedResult<LedgerEntryDto>>(&serialised) {\n error!(\"cache deserialise failed: {}\", e);\n   267→                }\n   268→            }\n   269→        }\n   270→\n   271→        // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n   272→        // Repository calls (CQRS read side)\n   273→        // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n   274→        let (offset, limit) = paging.offset_limit();\n   275→        let total_items = self\n   276→            .repo\n   277→            .count_entries(auth.tenant_id)\n   278→            .await\n   279→            .map_err(|e| LedgerEntryServiceError::Repository(e.to_string()))?;\n   280→\n   281→        let list = LedgerEntryServiceError::Repository(e.to_string()))?;\n   286→\n   287→        let dto_list: Vec<LedgerEntryDto> = list.into_iter().map(Into::into).collect();\n   288→\n   289→        let total_pages = ((total_items as f64) / (paging.per_page as f64)).ceil() as u32;\n   290→\n   291→        let response = PagedResult {\n   292→            data: dto_list,\n   293→            pagination: PaginationMeta {\n   294→                page: paging.page,\n   295→                per_page: paging.per_page,\n   296→                total_items,\n   297→                total_pages,\n   298→            },\n   299→            cache: CacheHint::Ttl(ttl_seconds),\n   300→        };\n   301→\n   302→        // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n   303→        // Store in cache for next caller\n   304→        // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n   305→        if let Ok(bytes) = bincode::serialize(&response) {\n   306→            if let Err(e) = self.cache.set_bin(&cache_key, &bytes, ttl_seconds).await {\n   307→                error!(\"failed to set cache {}: {}\", cache_key, e);\n   308→            }\n   309→        }\n   310→\n   311→        info!(\n   312→            \"tenant {} fetched ledger entries page {} ({} items)\",\n   313→            auth.tenant_id, paging.page, response.data.len()\n   314→        );\n   315→\n   316→        Ok(response)\n   317→    }\n   318→}\n   319→\n   320→// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   321→// Utility: Pretty debug printing for PaginationRequest\n   322→// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n   323→impl fmt::Display for PaginationRequest {\n   324→    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut LedgerEntry query service (read‐only).\n//!\n//! This module demonstrates how the project’s “view → view‐model → service layer”\n//! pipeline can be implemented for a single domain concept: `LedgerEntry`.\n//!\n//! Key features demonstrated\n//! -------------------------\n//! • Pagination helper with strict input validation  \n//! • Typed DTO + serde (for both REST & GraphQL payload reuse)  \n//! • Repository pattern & async/await friendliness  \n//! • Response-caching hints (for gateway & CDN layers)  \n//! • Centralised, structured error envelopes (thiserror)  \n//! • Integration hooks for authentication & structured logging  \n//!\n//! The implementation is intentionally self-contained so it can compile in\n//! isolation during CI.  External modules (e.g. `db`, `cache`, `auth`) are\n//! modeled with minimal trait stubs that the real microservice supplies.\n\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Domain Model (persisted)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct LedgerEntry {\n    pub id: Uuid,\n    pub tenant_id: Uuid,\n    pub account_id: entity.currency,\n            narrative: entity.narrative,\n            booked_at: entity.booked_at,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// MAX_PAGE_SIZE: u32 = 250;\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct PaginationRequest {\n    pub page: u32,\n    pub per_page: u32,\n}\n\nimpl PaginationRequest {\n    pub fn validate(self) -> Result<Self, LedgerEntryServiceError> {\n        if self.per_page == 0 || self.page == 0 {\n            return Err(LedgerEntryServiceError::Validation(\n                \"page and per_page must be > 0\".into(),\n            ));\n        }\n        if self.per_page > MAX_PAGE_SIZE {\n            return Err(LedgerEntryServiceError::Validation(format!(\n                \"per_page must not exceed {}\",\n                MAX_PAGE_SIZE\n            )));\n        }\n        Ok(self)\n    }\n\n    pub fn offset_limit(self) -> (u64, u64) {\n        let offset = (self.page.saturating_sub(1) * self.per_page) as u64;\n        (offset, self.per_page as u64)\n    }\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]\npub struct PaginationMeta {\n    pub page: u32,\n    pub per_page: u32,\n    pub total_items: u64,\n    pub total_pages: u32,\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Cache hints (propagated via response headers at gateway level)\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize)]\n#[serde(tag = \"strategy\", content = \"value\")]\npub enum CacheHint {\n    /// Force no-store (e.g. data that depends on auth scope)\n    NoCache,\n    /// Standard TTL in seconds\n    Ttl(u64),\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Error envelope\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n#[derive(Debug, NotFound,\n    #[error(\"repository error: {0}\")]\n    Repository(String),\n    #[error(\"internal: {0}\")]\n    Internal(String),\n}\n\n// {\n    pub fn new(repo: Arc<R>, cache: Arc<C>) -> Self {\n        Self { repo, cache }\n    }\n\n    fn make_cache_key(tenant: Uuid, paging: PaginationRequest) -> String {\n        format!(\n C>\nwhere\n    R: LedgerEntryRepository + 'static,\n    C: CacheBackend + 'static,\n{\n    async fn list_entries(\n        &self,\n        auth: Claims,\n        paging: PaginationRequest,\n    ) -> Result<PagedResult<LedgerEntryDto>, LedgerEntryServiceError> {\n        // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n        // AuthN / AuthZ\n        // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n        if !auth.can_read_ledger() {\n            error!(\"unauthorised access by user {}\", auth.user_id);\n            return ------------------------------------------------------------------\n        let paging = paging.validate()?;\n\n        let cache_key = Self::make_cache_key(auth.tenant_id, paging);\n        let ------------------------------------------------------------------\n        if let Ok(Some(serialised)) = self.cache.get_bin(&cache_key).await {\n            trace!(\"cache hit for {}\", cache_key);\n .map_err(|e| LedgerEntryServiceError::Repository(e.to_string()))?;\n\n        let dto_list: Vec<LedgerEntryDto> = list.into_iter().map(Into::into).collect();\n\n        let total_pages = ((total_items               per_page: paging.per_page,\n                total_items,\n                total_pages,\n            },\n            cache: CacheHint::Ttl(ttl_seconds),\n        };\n\n        // cache_key, e);\n            }\n        }\n\n        info!(\n            \"tenant {} fetched ledger entries page {} ({} items)\",\n            auth.tenant_id, paging.page, response.data.len()\n        );\n\n        Ok(response)\n    }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Utility: Pretty debug printing for PaginationRequest\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\nimpl fmt::Display for PaginationRequest {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        write!(f, \"page={}, per_page={}\", self.page, self.per_page)\n    }\n}\n\n// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Tests (can be executed with `cargo Serialize)]\npub struct Paginated<T> {\n    pub data: Vec<T>,\n    pub page: u32,\n    pub per_page: u16,\n    pub total_items: u64,\n    pub total_pages: u32,\n}\n\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n//  AUTHENTICATION MIDDLEWARE //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct JwtAuth {\n    pub tenant_id: Uuid,\n    pub user_id: Uuid,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum AuthError {\n    #[error(\"Unauthorized\")]\n    Unauthorized,\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl<S> FromRequestParts<S> for JwtAuth\nwhere\n    S: Send + Sync,\n{\n    type Rejection = ApiError;\n\n    async fn from_request_parts(parts: &mut Parts, _state: &S) -> Result<Self, Self::Rejection> {\n        let TypedHeader(Authorization(bearer)) =\n            TypedHeader::<Authorization<Bearer>>::from_request_parts(parts, _state)\n                .await\n                .map_err(|_| ApiError::unauthorized())?;\n\n        // In real life, validate signature & claims with proper key rotation\n        let token_str = bearer.token();\n\n        // For demonstration, we expect \"tenant:<tenant_id>:user:<user_id>\"\n        let mut iter = token_str.split(':');\n        let prefix = iter.next().unwrap_or_default();\n        if prefix != \"tenant\" {\n            return Err(ApiError::unauthorized());\n        }\n        let tenant_id = iter\n            .next()\n            .and_then(|s| Uuid::parse_str(s).ok())\n            .ok_or_else(ApiError::unauthorized)?;\n\n        let prefix2 = iter.next().unwrap_or_default();\n        if prefix2 != \"user\" {\n            return Err(ApiError::unauthorized());\n        }\n        let user_id = iter\n            .next()\n            .and_then(|s| Uuid::parse_str(s).ok())\n            .ok_or_else(ApiError::unauthorized)?;\n\n        Ok(Self { tenant_id, user_id })\n    }\n}\n\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n//  REPOSITORY /////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\n#[async_trait]\npub trait LedgerEntryRepository: Send + Sync {\n    async fn list(\n        &self,\n        tenant_id: Uuid,\n        query: &ListQuery,\n    ) -> Result<(Vec<LedgerEntry>, u64), RepositoryError>;\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum RepositoryError {\n    #[error(\"DB error: {0}\")]\n    Db(#[from] sqlx::Error),\n}\n\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n//  SQLx IMPLEMENTATION ////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\npub struct PgLedgerEntryRepo {\n    pool: PgPool,\n}\n\nimpl PgLedgerEntryRepo {\n    pub fn new(pool: PgPool) -> Self {\n        Self { pool }\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl LedgerEntryRepository for PgLedgerEntryRepo {\n    #[instrument(skip(self, query))]\n    async fn list(\n        &self,\n        tenant_id: Uuid,\n        query: &ListQuery,\n    ) -> Result<(Vec<LedgerEntry>, u64), RepositoryError> {\n        let page = query.page.unwrap_or(1);\n        let per_page = query.per_page.unwrap_or(50) as i64;\n        let offset = ((page - 1) as i64) * per_page;\n\n        // Filtering\n        let (entries, total): (Vec<LedgerEntry>, i64) = if let Some(ref account) = query.account_code\n        {\n            let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, LedgerEntry>(\n                r#\"\n                SELECT *\n                FROM ledger_entries\n                WHERE tenant_id = $1\n                  AND account_code = $2\n                ORDER BY occurred_at DESC\n                OFFSET $3\n                LIMIT $4\n                \"#,\n            )\n            .bind(tenant_id)\n            .bind(account)\n            .bind(offset)\n            .bind(per_page)\n            .fetch_all(&self.pool)\n            .await?;\n\n            let total: (i64,) = sqlx::query_as(\n                r#\"\n                SELECT COUNT(*) as count\n                FROM ledger_entries\n                WHERE tenant_id = $1\n                  AND account_code = $2\n                \"#,\n            )\n            .bind(tenant_id)\n            .bind(account)\n            .fetch_one(&self.pool)\n            .await?;\n\n            (rows, total.0)\n        } else {\n            let rows = sqlx::query_as::<_, LedgerEntry>(\n                r#\"\n                SELECT *\n                FROM ledger_entries\n                WHERE tenant_id = $1\n                ORDER BY occurred_at DESC\n                OFFSET $2\n                LIMIT $3\n                \"#,\n            )\n            .bind(tenant_id)\n            .bind(offset)\n            .bind(per_page)\n            .fetch_all(&self.pool)\n            .await?;\n\n            let total: (i64,) = sqlx::query_as(\n                r#\"\n                SELECT COUNT(*) as count\n                FROM ledger_entries\n                WHERE tenant_id = $1\n                \"#,\n            )\n            .bind(tenant_id)\n            .fetch_one(&self.pool)\n            .await?;\n\n            (rows, total.0)\n        };\n\n        Ok((entries, total as u64))\n    }\n}\n\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n//  SERVICE LAYER /////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\npub struct LedgerEntryQuerySvc<R> {\n    repo: Arc<R>,\n    redis: RedisPool,\n}\n\nimpl<R> LedgerEntryQuerySvc<R>\nwhere\n    R: LedgerEntryRepository + 'static,\n{\n    pub fn new(repo: Arc<R>, redis: RedisPool) -> Self {\n        Self { repo, redis }\n    }\n\n    fn cache_key(tenant: Uuid, query: &ListQuery) -> String {\n        let filter = query.account_code.as_deref().unwrap_or(\"_\");\n        let page = query.page.unwrap_or(1);\n        let per_page = query.per_page.unwrap_or(50);\n        format!(\"ledger_entries:{tenant}:{filter}:{page}:{per_page}\")\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    pub async fn list(\n        &self,\n        tenant: Uuid,\n        query: ListQuery,\n    ) -> Result<Paginated<LedgerEntryDto>, ServiceError> {\n        // 1. Try cache (30ms timeout to avoid head-of-line blocking)\n        let key = Self::cache_key(tenant, &query);\n        if let Ok(Some(raw)) = self.try_fetch_cache(&key).await {\n            debug!(%tenant, %key, \"Cache hit\");\n            return serde_json::from_slice::<Paginated<LedgerEntryDto>>(&raw)\n                .map_err(ServiceError::CacheSerialization);\n        }\n\n        // 2. Query repository\n        debug!(%tenant, \"Cache miss – querying DB\");\n        let (entries, total) = self.repo.list(tenant, &query).await?;\n        let dto: Vec<LedgerEntryDto> = entries.into_iter().map(Into::into).collect();\n\n        let page = query.page.unwrap_or(1);\n        let per_page = query.per_page.unwrap_or(50);\n        let total_pages = ((total as f64) / (per_page as f64)).ceil() as u32;\n\n        let payload = Paginated {\n            data: dto,\n            page,\n            per_page,\n            total_items: total,\n            total_pages,\n        };\n\n        // 3. Backfill cache (best-effort)\n        let _ = self.persist_cache(&key, &payload).await;\n\n        Ok(payload)\n    }\n\n    async fn try_fetch_cache(&self, key: &str) -> Result<Option<Vec<u8>>, ServiceError> {\n        let mut conn: RedisConn = self.redis.get().await?;\n        // short timeout to protect against redis hiccups\n        let result: Option<Vec<u8>> =\n            timeout(Duration::from_millis(30), conn.get(key)).await??;\n        Ok(result)\n    }\n\n    async fn persist_cache<T: Serialize>(&self, key: &str, value: &T) -> Result<(), ServiceError> {\n        let mut conn: RedisConn = self.redis.get().await?;\n        let data = serde_json::to_vec(value)?;\n        let _: () = conn\n            .set_ex(key, data, 60) // 60 seconds TTL\n            .await\n            .map_err(ServiceError::Cache)?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum ServiceError {\n    #[error(\"repository error: {0}\")]\n    Repo(#[from] RepositoryError),\n\n    #[error(\"redis error: {0}\")]\n    Cache(#[from] deadpool_redis::redis::RedisError),\n\n    #[error(\"serialization error: {0}\")]\n    CacheSerialization(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n}\n\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n//  API HANDLER ////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\n#[instrument(skip_all, fields(tenant=%auth.tenant_id, user=%auth.user_id))]\npub async fn list_ledger_entries_handler<R>(\n    auth: JwtAuth,\n    Extension(svc): Extension<Arc<LedgerEntryQuerySvc<R>>>,\n    headers: HeaderMap,\n    Query(mut query): Query<ListQuery>,\n) -> Result<Response, ApiError>\nwhere\n    R: LedgerEntryRepository + 'static,\n{\n    // Merge defaults\n    if query.page.is_none() {\n        query.page = Some(1);\n    }\n    if query.per_page.is_none() {\n        query.per_page = Some(50);\n    }\n\n    // Validate\n    query\n        .validate()\n        .map_err(|e| ApiError::validation(\"query\", e.to_string()))?;\n\n    let result = svc.list(auth.tenant_id, query).await?;\n    let json = Json(result);\n\n    // Add pagination hints as response headers\n    let mut resp = json.into_response();\n    {\n        let headers = resp.headers_mut();\n        headers.insert(\"x-total-items\", result.total_items.into());\n        headers.insert(\"x-total-pages\", result.total_pages.into());\n    }\n    Ok(resp)\n}\n\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n//  API ROUTER FACTORY /////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\npub fn get(list_ledger_entries_handler::<R>))\n        .layer(Extension(svc))\n}\n\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n//  API-LEVEL ERROR Serialize)]\nstruct ErrorEnvelope<'a> {\n    code: &'a str,\n    message: &'a str,\n    details: Option<String>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum ApiError {\n    #[error(\"validation {\n    fn validation(field: &'static str, message: String) -> Self {\n        Self::Validation { field, message }\n    }\n\n    fn unauthorized() -> Self {\n        Self::Unauthorized\n    }\n}\n\nimpl From<ServiceError> for ApiError {\n    fn from(e: ServiceError) -> Self {\n        match e {\n            ServiceError::Repo(repo_err) => {\n                error!(error = %repo_err, layer trouble\");\n                // Degrade gracefully – still return 200 with DB data if possible.\n                ApiError::Internal\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl IntoResponse for StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,\n                ErrorEnvelope {\n                    code: \"validation_error\",\n                    message: \"Payload validation failed\",\n                    details: Some(format!(\"{field}: {message}\")),\n                },\n            ),\n            ApiError::Unauthorized => (\n                StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED,\n                ErrorEnvelope {\n                    code: \"unauthorized\",\n                    message: \"Authorization required\",\n                    details: None,\n                },\n            ),\n            ApiError::Internal => (\n                StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n                ErrorEnvelope {\n                    code: \"internal_error\",\n                    message: \"Unexpected server error\",\n                    details: None,\n                },\n            ),\n        };\n\n        let body = Json(envelope).into_response().into_body();\n        (status, body).into_response()\n    }\n}\n\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n//  DISPLAY HELPERS
