<= `to` date\")]\n    InvalidDateRange,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum RepoError {\n    #[error(\"database error: {0}\")]\n    Db(#[from] anyhow::Error),\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum CacheError {\n    #[error(\"redis error: {0}\")]\n    Redis(#[from] redis::RedisError),\n    #[error(\"serialization error: {0}\")]\n    Serde(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum ServiceError {\n    #[error(\"validation error: {0}\")]\n    Validation(#[from] ValidationError),\n    #[error(\"repository error: {0}\")]\n    Repository(#[from] RepoError),\n    #[cfg(feature = \"response_caching\")]\n    #[error(\"cache backend error: {0}\")]\n    Cache(#[from] CacheError),\n}\n```\n\n### Pattern 4: API Error with IntoResponse (module_44.txt)\n\n```rust\n// File: /app/project/src/module_44.txt\n// Lines: 489-559\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum ApiError {\n    #[error(\"validation error\")]\n    Validation { field: &'static str, message: String },\n\n    #[error(\"authentication failed\")]\n    Unauthorized,\n\n    #[error(\"internal error\")]\n    Internal,\n}\n\nimpl IntoResponse for ApiError {\n    fn into_response(self) -> Response {\n        let (status, envelope) = match &self {\n            ApiError::Validation { field, message } => (\n                StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,\n                ErrorEnvelope {\n                    code: \"validation_error\",\n                    message: \"Payload validation failed\",\n                    details: Some(format!(\"{field}: {message}\")),\n                },\n            ),\n            ApiError::Unauthorized => (\n                StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED,\n                ErrorEnvelope {\n                    code: \"unauthorized\",\n                    message: \"Authorization required\",\n                    details: None,\n                },\n            ),\n            ApiError::Internal => (\n                StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n                ErrorEnvelope {\n                    code: \"internal_error\",\n                    message: \"Unexpected server error\",\n                    details: None,\n                },\n            ),\n        };\n\n        let body = Json(envelope).into_response().into_body();\n        (status, body).into_response()\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Pattern 5: Repository Result with anyhow::Error (module_3.txt)\n\n```rust\n// File: /app/project/src/module_3.txt\n// Lines: 162-175\n#[async_trait]\npub trait LedgerEntryRepository: Send + Sync {\n    async fn count_entries(\n        &self,\n        tenant_id: Uuid,\n    ) -> Result<u64, anyhow::Error>;\n\n    async fn list_entries(\n        &self,\n        tenant_id: Uuid,\n        offset: u64,\n        limit: u64,\n    ) -> Result<Vec<LedgerEntry>, anyhow::Error>;\n}\n```\n\n## Analysis\n\n### Error Handling Patterns Discovered\n\nThe LedgerLink Nexus codebase exhibits **12 distinct error handling patterns** across 85+ modules:\n\n1. **thiserror-derived enums** (dominant pattern, 80+ modules)\n2. **Result<T, anyhow::Error>** for trait methods\n3. **Result<T, Box<dyn std::error::Error>>** for generic conversions\n4. **derive_more enums** with manual Error impl\n5. **Multi-layered From implementations**\n6. **Repository-specific error wrappers**\n7. **API-level IntoResponse implementations**\n8. **Authentication/Authorization error variants**\n9. **Configuration loading errors**\n10. **Cache-specific error hierarchies**\n11. **Validation error wrapping**\n12. **Layered service error stacks**\n\n### Error Type Diversity\n\n| Error Type Name | Occurrences | HTTP Status Mapping |\n|-----------------|-------------|---------------------|\n| `ServiceError` | 15+ modules | 400/500 |\n| `RepositoryError` | 10+ modules | 500 |\n| `CacheError` | 5+ modules | 500 |\n| `ValidationError` | 8+ modules | 400 |\n| `ApiError` | 5+ modules | 400/401/403/500 |\n| `AuthError` | 5+ modules | 401 |\n| `LedgerEntryServiceError` | 3 modules | 400/401/404/500 |\n\n### Architectural Assessment\n\nThe current error handling has several issues:\n- **Inconsistent naming**: Same logical errors have different names across modules\n- **Duplicate definitions**: `CacheError` defined in 5+ places with slight variations\n- **Non-uniform HTTP mapping**: Same error type maps to different status codes\n- **Missing centralization**: No single source of truth for error types\n- **Test brittleness**: Tests check for different error formats\n\n## Implementation (For Code Modification Tasks)\n\n### New File: `/app/project/src/error.txt` (or `error.rs`)\n\nThe unified `AppError` enum consolidates all error handling:\n\n```rust\n//! src/error.rs\n//!\n//! Unified application-wide error handling for LedgerLink Nexus.\n\nuse axum::{\n    http::StatusCode,\n    response::{IntoResponse, Response},\n    Json,\n};\nuse serde::Serialize;\nuse thiserror::Error;\n\n/// Structured JSON error response body.\n#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\npub struct ErrorResponse {\n    pub error: ErrorBody,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\npub struct ErrorBody {\n    #[serde(rename = \"type\")]\n    pub error_type: String,\n    pub message: String,\n}\n\nimpl ErrorResponse {\n    pub fn new(error_type: impl Into<String>, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            error: ErrorBody {\n                error_type: error_type.into(),\n                message: message.into(),\n            },\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Unified application error type for LedgerLink Nexus.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum AppError {\n    /// Validation error for invalid user input.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 400 Bad Request.\n    #[error(\"Validation Error: {0}\")]\n    ValidationError(String),\n\n    /// Resource not found error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 404 Not Found.\n    #[error(\"Resource Not Found: {0}\")]\n    NotFound(String),\n\n    /// Authentication failure.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 401 Unauthorized.\n    #[error(\"Authentication Failed\")]\n    Unauthorized,\n\n    /// Authorization failure - user lacks permissions.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 403 Forbidden.\n    #[error(\"Access Forbidden: {0}\")]\n    Forbidden(String),\n\n    /// Database operation error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Database Error: {0}\")]\n    Database(#[from] sqlx::Error),\n\n    /// Redis caching error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Cache Error: {0}\")]\n    Cache(#[from] redis::RedisError),\n\n    /// JSON serialization/deserialization error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Serialization Error: {0}\")]\n    Serialization(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n\n    /// Validation errors from the `validator` crate.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 400 Bad Request.\n    #[error(\"Input Validation Failed: {0}\")]\n    ValidatorErrors(#[from] validator::ValidationErrors),\n\n    /// Generic internal server error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Internal Server Error: {0}\")]\n    Internal(#[from] anyhow::Error),\n\n    /// Pagination error for invalid page/limit parameters.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 400 Bad Request.\n    #[error(\"Pagination Error: {0}\")]\n    PaginationError(String),\n\n    /// Configuration error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Configuration Error: {0}\")]\n    ConfigError(String),\n\n    /// External service error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 502 Bad Gateway.\n    #[error(\"External Service Error: {0}\")]\n    ExternalService(String),\n\n    /// Request timeout error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 504 Gateway Timeout.\n    #[error(\"Request Timeout: {0}\")]\n    Timeout(String),\n\n    /// Rate limit exceeded.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 429 Too Many Requests.\n    #[error(\"Rate Limit Exceeded: {0}\")]\n    RateLimited(String),\n\n    /// Conflict error (e.g., duplicate resource).\n    /// Maps to HTTP 409 Conflict.\n    #[error(\"Conflict: {0}\")]\n    Conflict(String),\n}\n\nimpl AppError {\n    /// Returns the HTTP status code for this error.\n    pub fn status_code(&self) -> StatusCode {\n        match self {\n            AppError::ValidationError(_) => StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,\n            AppError::ValidatorErrors(_) => StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,\n            AppError::PaginationError(_) => StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,\n            AppError::NotFound(_) => StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,\n            AppError::Unauthorized => StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED,\n            AppError::Forbidden(_) => StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,\n            AppError::Conflict(_) => StatusCode::CONFLICT,\n            AppError::RateLimited(_) => StatusCode::TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,\n            AppError::Timeout(_) => StatusCode::GATEWAY_TIMEOUT,\n            AppError::ExternalService(_) => StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY,\n            AppError::Database(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n            AppError::Cache(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n            AppError::Serialization(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n            AppError::Internal(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n            AppError::ConfigError(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the error type string for the JSON response.\n    pub fn error_type(&self) -> &'static str {\n        match self {\n            AppError::ValidationError(_) => \"validation\",\n            AppError::ValidatorErrors(_) => \"validation\",\n            AppError::PaginationError(_) => \"pagination\",\n            AppError::NotFound(_) => \"not_found\",\n            AppError::Unauthorized => \"unauthorized\",\n            AppError::Forbidden(_) => \"forbidden\",\n            AppError::Conflict(_) => \"conflict\",\n            AppError::RateLimited(_) => \"rate_limited\",\n            AppError::Timeout(_) => \"timeout\",\n            AppError::ExternalService(_) => \"external_service\",\n            AppError::Database(_) => \"database\",\n            AppError::Cache(_) => \"cache\",\n            AppError::Serialization(_) => \"serialization\",\n            AppError::Internal(_) => \"internal\",\n            AppError::ConfigError(_) => \"config\",\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a validation error.\n    pub fn validation(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::ValidationError(msg.into())\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a not found error.\n    pub fn not_found(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::NotFound(msg.into())\n    }\n\n    /// Creates an internal error from any error type.\n    pub fn internal(err: impl std::error::Error + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::Error::new(err))\n    }\n\n    /// Creates an internal error from a string message.\n    pub fn internal_msg(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::anyhow!(msg.into()))\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a forbidden error.\n    pub fn forbidden(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::Forbidden(msg.into())\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a pagination error.\n    pub fn pagination(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::PaginationError(msg.into())\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a conflict error.\n    pub fn conflict(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::Conflict(msg.into())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Implement `IntoResponse` for `AppError` to convert errors into HTTP responses.\nimpl IntoResponse for AppError {\n    fn into_response(self) -> Response {\n        tracing::error!(\n            error_type = self.error_type(),\n            error_message = %self,\n            \"API error occurred\"\n        );\n\n        let status = self.status_code();\n        let error_response = ErrorResponse::new(self.error_type(), self.to_string());\n\n        (status, Json(error_response)).into_response()\n    }\n}\n\n/// Convenience type alias for Results using AppError.\npub type AppResult<T> = Result<T, AppError>;\n\n// Additional From implementations\nimpl From<String> for AppError {\n    fn from(msg: String) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::anyhow!(msg))\n    }\n}\n\nimpl From<&str> for AppError {\n    fn from(msg: &str) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::anyhow!(msg.to_string()))\n    }\n}\n\nimpl From<std::io::Error> for AppError {\n    fn from(err: std::io::Error) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::Error::new(err))\n    }\n}\n\nimpl From<Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> for AppError {\n    fn from(err: Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::Error::new(err))\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_validation_error_status_code() {\n        let err = AppError::ValidationError(\"test\".to_string());\n        assert_eq!(err.status_code(), StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST);\n        assert_eq!(err.error_type(), \"validation\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_not_found_error_status_code() {\n        let err = AppError::NotFound(\"resource\".to_string());\n        assert_eq!(err.status_code(), StatusCode::NOT_FOUND);\n        assert_eq!(err.error_type(), \"not_found\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_unauthorized_error_status_code() {\n        let err = AppError::Unauthorized;\n        assert_eq!(err.status_code(), StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED);\n        assert_eq!(err.error_type(), \"unauthorized\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_error_response_serialization() {\n        let response = ErrorResponse::new(\"validation\", \"test error\");\n        let json = serde_json::to_string(&response).unwrap();\n        assert!(json.contains(\"\\\"type\\\":\\\"validation\\\"\"));\n        assert!(json.contains(\"\\\"message\\\":\\\"test error\\\"\"));\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Refactored: `/app/project/src/module_43.txt`\n\n**Changes made:**\n\n1. **Remove local error definitions** - StatusCode,\n    expected_type: &str,\n) -> Result<()> {\n    assert_eq!(resp.status(), expected_status);\n\n    let body: serde_json::Value = json(resp.clone()).await?;\n\n    let error_type = body\n        .pointer(\"/error/type\")\n        .and_then(|v| v.as_str())\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"Missing error.type in response\"))?;\n\n    assert_eq!(error_type, expected_type, \"Error type handling patterns across 85+ modules\n\n- **Key components**:\n  - `/app/project/src/error.txt` (new) - Centralized AppError enum with thiserror, From traits, IntoResponse\n  - `/app/project/src/module_43.txt` - RepositoryError, CacheError, ServiceError refactored\n  - `/app/project/src/module_8.txt` - PageError, RepositoryError, ServiceError refactored\n  - updated for new error structure\n\n- **Architectural pattern**:\n  - **Centralized error type** with `#[derive(Debug, Error)]` from thiserror\n  - **Automatic From conversions** via `#[from]` attributes for sqlx::Error, redis::RedisError, serde_json::Error, validator::ValidationErrors, anyhow::Error\n  - **HTTP response mapping** via `IntoResponse` trait implementation with consistent JSON structure: `{\"error\": {\"type\": \"...\", \"message\": \"...\"}}`\n  - **Type alias** `AppResult<T>` for `Result<T, AppError>`\n\n- **Recommendations**:\n  1. Create `src/error.rs` with the unified `AppError` enum before modifying modules\n  2. Use incremental refactoring: start with one module (e.g., module_8), verify tests pass, then expand\n  3. Update all module imports to `use crate::error::{AppError, AppResult};`\n  4. Remove all local error type definitions after confirming functionality\n  5. File: /app/project/src/module_44.txt\n// Lines: 489-559\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum ApiError {\n    #[error(\"validation error\")]\n    Validation { field: &'static str, message: String },\n\n    #[error(\"authentication failed\")]\n    Unauthorized,\n\n    #[error(\"internal error\")]\n    Internal,\n}\n\nimpl IntoResponse for ApiError {\n    fn into_response(self) -> Response {\n        let (status, envelope) = match &self {\n            ApiError::Validation { field, message } => (\n                StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,\n                ErrorEnvelope {\n                    code: \"validation_error\",\n                    message: \"Payload validation failed\",\n                    details: Some(format!(\"{field}: {message}\")),\n                },\n            ),\n            ApiError::Unauthorized => (\n                StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED,\n                ErrorEnvelope {\n                    code: \"unauthorized\",\n                    message: \"Authorization required\",\n                    details: None,\n                },\n            ),\n            ApiError::Internal => (\n                StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n                ErrorEnvelope {\n                    code: \"internal_error\",\n                    message: \"Unexpected server error\",\n                    details: None,\n                },\n            ),\n        };\n\n        let body = Json(envelope).into_response().into_body();\n        (status, body).into_response()\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Pattern 5: Repository Result with anyhow::Error modules:\n\n1. **thiserror-derived enums** (dominant pattern, 80+ modules)\n2. **Result<T, anyhow::Error>** for trait methods\n3. **Result<T, Box<dyn std::error::Error>>** for generic conversions\n4. **derive_more enums** with manual Error impl\n5. **Multi-layered From implementations**\n6. **Repository-specific error wrappers**\n7. **API-level IntoResponse implementations**\n8. **Authentication/Authorization error variants**\n9. **Configuration loading errors**\n10. **Cache-specific error hierarchies**\n11. **Validation error wrapping**\n12. **Layered service error stacks**\n\n### Error Type Diversity\n\n| Error Type Name | Occurrences | HTTP Status Mapping |\n|-----------------|-------------|---------------------|\n| `ServiceError` | 15+ modules | 400/500 |\n| `RepositoryError` | 10+ modules | 500 |\n| `CacheError` | 5+ modules | 500 |\n| `ValidationError` | 8+ modules | 400 |\n| `ApiError` | 5+ modules | 400/401/403/500 |\n| `AuthError` | 5+ modules | 401 |\n| `LedgerEntryServiceError` | 3 modules | 400/401/404/500 |\n\n### Architectural Assessment\n\nThe current error handling has several issues:\n- **Inconsistent naming**: Same logical errors have different names across modules\n- **Duplicate definitions**: `CacheError` defined in 5+ places with slight variations\n- **Non-uniform HTTP mapping**: Same error type maps to different status codes\n- **Missing centralization**: No single source of truth for error types\n- **Test brittleness**: Tests check for different error formats\n\n## Implementation (For Code Modification Tasks)\n\n### New File: `/app/project/src/error.txt` (or `error.rs`)\n\nThe unified `AppError` enum consolidates all error handling:\n\n```rust\n//! src/error.rs\n//!\n//! Unified application-wide error handling for LedgerLink Nexus.\n\nuse axum::{\n    http::StatusCode,\n    response::{IntoResponse, Response},\n    Json,\n};\nuse serde::Serialize;\nuse thiserror::Error;\n\n/// Structured JSON error response body.\n#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\npub struct ErrorResponse {\n    pub error: ErrorBody,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\npub struct ErrorBody {\n    #[serde(rename = \"type\")]\n    pub error_type: String,\n    pub message: String,\n}\n\nimpl ErrorResponse {\n    pub fn new(error_type: impl Into<String>, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            error: ErrorBody {\n                error_type: error_type.into(),\n                message: message.into(),\n            },\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Unified application error type for LedgerLink Nexus.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum AppError {\n    /// Validation error for invalid user input.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 400 Bad Request.\n    #[error(\"Validation Error: {0}\")]\n    ValidationError(String),\n\n    /// Resource not found error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 404 Not Found.\n    #[error(\"Resource Not Found: {0}\")]\n    NotFound(String),\n\n    /// Authentication failure.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 401 Unauthorized.\n    #[error(\"Authentication Failed\")]\n    Unauthorized,\n\n    /// Authorization failure - user lacks permissions.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 403 Forbidden.\n    #[error(\"Access Forbidden: {0}\")]\n    Forbidden(String),\n\n    /// Database operation error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Database Error: {0}\")]\n    Database(#[from] sqlx::Error),\n\n    /// Redis caching error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Cache Error: {0}\")]\n    Cache(#[from] redis::RedisError),\n\n    /// JSON serialization/deserialization error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Serialization Error: {0}\")]\n    Serialization(#[from] serde_json::Error),\n\n    /// Validation errors from the `validator` crate.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 400 Bad Request.\n    #[error(\"Input Validation Failed: {0}\")]\n    ValidatorErrors(#[from] validator::ValidationErrors),\n\n    /// Generic internal server error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Internal Server Error: {0}\")]\n    Internal(#[from] anyhow::Error),\n\n    /// Pagination error for invalid page/limit parameters.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 400 Bad Request.\n    #[error(\"Pagination Error: {0}\")]\n    PaginationError(String),\n\n    /// Configuration error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 500 Internal Server Error.\n    #[error(\"Configuration Error: {0}\")]\n    ConfigError(String),\n\n    /// External service error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 502 Bad Gateway.\n    #[error(\"External Service Error: {0}\")]\n    ExternalService(String),\n\n    /// Request timeout error.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 504 Gateway Timeout.\n    #[error(\"Request Timeout: {0}\")]\n    Timeout(String),\n\n    /// Rate limit exceeded.\n    /// Maps to HTTP 429 Too Many Requests.\n    #[error(\"Rate Limit Exceeded: {0}\")]\n    RateLimited(String),\n\n    /// Conflict error (e.g., duplicate resource).\n    /// Maps to HTTP 409 Conflict.\n    #[error(\"Conflict: {0}\")]\n    Conflict(String),\n}\n\nimpl AppError {\n    /// Returns the HTTP status code for this error.\n    pub fn status_code(&self) -> StatusCode {\n        match self {\n            AppError::ValidationError(_) => StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,\n            AppError::ValidatorErrors(_) => StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,\n            AppError::PaginationError(_) => StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST,\n            AppError::NotFound(_) => StatusCode::NOT_FOUND,\n            AppError::Unauthorized => StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED,\n            AppError::Forbidden(_) => StatusCode::FORBIDDEN,\n            AppError::Conflict(_) => StatusCode::CONFLICT,\n            AppError::RateLimited(_) => StatusCode::TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,\n            AppError::Timeout(_) => StatusCode::GATEWAY_TIMEOUT,\n            AppError::ExternalService(_) => StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY,\n            AppError::Database(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n            AppError::Cache(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n            AppError::Serialization(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n            AppError::Internal(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n            AppError::ConfigError(_) => StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Returns the error type string for the JSON response.\n    pub fn error_type(&self) -> &'static str {\n        match self {\n            AppError::ValidationError(_) => \"validation\",\n            AppError::ValidatorErrors(_) => \"validation\",\n            AppError::PaginationError(_) => \"pagination\",\n            AppError::NotFound(_) => \"not_found\",\n            AppError::Unauthorized => \"unauthorized\",\n            AppError::Forbidden(_) => \"forbidden\",\n            AppError::Conflict(_) => \"conflict\",\n            AppError::RateLimited(_) => \"rate_limited\",\n            AppError::Timeout(_) => \"timeout\",\n            AppError::ExternalService(_) => \"external_service\",\n            AppError::Database(_) => \"database\",\n            AppError::Cache(_) => \"cache\",\n            AppError::Serialization(_) => \"serialization\",\n            AppError::Internal(_) => \"internal\",\n            AppError::ConfigError(_) => \"config\",\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a validation error.\n    pub fn validation(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::ValidationError(msg.into())\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a not found error.\n    pub fn not_found(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::NotFound(msg.into())\n    }\n\n    /// Creates an internal error from any error type.\n    pub fn internal(err: impl std::error::Error + Send + Sync + 'static) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::Error::new(err))\n    }\n\n    /// Creates an internal error from a string message.\n    pub fn internal_msg(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::anyhow!(msg.into()))\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a forbidden error.\n    pub fn forbidden(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::Forbidden(msg.into())\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a pagination error.\n    pub fn pagination(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::PaginationError(msg.into())\n    }\n\n    /// Creates a conflict error.\n    pub fn conflict(msg: impl Into<String>) -> Self {\n        AppError::Conflict(msg.into())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Implement `IntoResponse` for `AppError` to convert errors into HTTP responses.\nimpl IntoResponse for AppError {\n    fn into_response(self) -> Response {\n        tracing::error!(\n            error_type = self.error_type(),\n            error_message = %self,\n            \"API error occurred\"\n        );\n\n        let status = self.status_code();\n        let error_response = ErrorResponse::new(self.error_type(), self.to_string());\n\n        (status, Json(error_response)).into_response()\n    }\n}\n\n/// Convenience type alias for Results using AppError.\npub type AppResult<T> = Result<T, AppError>;\n\n// Additional From implementations\nimpl From<String> for AppError {\n    fn from(msg: String) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::anyhow!(msg))\n    }\n}\n\nimpl From<&str> for AppError {\n    fn from(msg: &str) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::anyhow!(msg.to_string()))\n    }\n}\n\nimpl From<std::io::Error> for AppError {\n    fn from(err: std::io::Error) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::Error::new(err))\n    }\n}\n\nimpl From<Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>> for AppError {\n    fn from(err: Box<dyn std::error::Error + Send + Sync>) -> Self {\n        AppError::Internal(anyhow::Error::new(err))\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_validation_error_status_code() {\n        let err = AppError::ValidationError(\"test\".to_string());\n        assert_eq!(err.status_code(), StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST);\n        assert_eq!(err.error_type(), \"validation\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_not_found_error_status_code() {\n        let err = AppError::NotFound(\"resource\".to_string());\n        assert_eq!(err.status_code(), StatusCode::NOT_FOUND);\n        assert_eq!(err.error_type(), \"not_found\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_unauthorized_error_status_code() {\n        let err = AppError::Unauthorized;\n        assert_eq!(err.status_code(), StatusCode::UNAUTHORIZED);\n        assert_eq!(err.error_type(), \"unauthorized\");\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_error_response_serialization() {\n        let response = ErrorResponse::new(\"validation\", \"test error\");\n        let json = serde_json::to_string(&response).unwrap();\n        assert!(json.contains(\"\\\"type\\\":\\\"validation\\\"\"));\n        assert!(json.contains(\"\\\"message\\\":\\\"test error\\\"\"));\n    }\n}\n```\n\n### Refactored: `/app/project/src/module_43.txt`\n\n**Changes made:**\n\n1. **Remove local error definitions** -
